# Critical pairs of the fusion rules

Generated by `met circuit rules --critical-pairs`; checked by the same command with `--check`. Do not edit.

A critical pair is two matches of the class's rules, over the circuit with no node claimed, that share a node. Each is resolved both ways: one match claimed first, then the normal greedy. Classes: `joinable-identical` (same plan digest), `joinable-same-cost` (same headroom floor), `priority-decided` (different floor: rule priority alone picks the plan), `forced` (only one order gives a full plan on the class's kernels). Pairs are deduplicated across layers by the site signature (rule, and each node's block, local id, layer kind and weight format); `sites` counts representatives, `occurrences` the layer instances they stand for. A floor is the step's headroom floor in microseconds, Σ max(bytes / bandwidth, FLOPs / peak) over its launch sites.

## Summary

310 plans.

| class | sites | occurrences |
|---|---:|---:|
| joinable-identical | 4 | 136 |
| joinable-same-cost | 1832 | 39140 |
| priority-decided | 639 | 14878 |
| forced | 749 | 19408 |

Priority-decided sites where today's plan takes the cheaper side: 601; the dearer side: 0; neither side: 38.

Same-cost sites whose two plans launch a different number of kernels (the floor carries no launch cost): 1473; today's plan takes the side with fewer launches at 1375, more at 34, neither side at 64.

Forced sites: 189 with one order refused (a seed whose rule that plan refuses, or a node left uncovered), 560 with one order leaving more nodes to placeholders.
